Research Report

2026年 11月 6日

1/7 
2014年 GPIF改革から考える国内債券の位置づけ 
～「金利のある世界」で現行配分は最適か～ 
 資産運用調査部 主席研究員  奥田 宏二

（要旨） 
○ GPIFが基本ポートフォリオを変更し、国内債券の割合を引き上げるのではないかとの観測が
市場で浮き沈みしている。本稿では、現在の基本ポートフォリオの起点となった 2014年の運
用改革の成立過程を分析し、変更する場合にどのようなことが起き得るのか考察する。 
○ 2014年改革では、基本ポートフォリオに占める国内債券を60％から 35％へ大幅に引き下げ、
国内外の株式を約 2倍に増やした。アベノミクスの象徴の一つとして GPIF改革は語られる
ことが多いが、改革につながる議論は民主党政権期から始まっていた。 
○ 当時のGPIF運用委員会の議事録によれば、株式をどこまで増やすかより、国内債券をどこま
で減らすかが議論の中心だった。また、当時決定した国内債券 35％という配分は、期待収益
率やリスクだけで機械的に決まったものではなく、移行コストや市場への影響などを考慮し
て決定された。市場への影響を抑えるため、正式決定前から乖離許容幅を使って国内債券を
減らしていたのは、今後の動向を見極めるうえで興味深い。当面、四半期の運用実績に注目
が集まるだろう。 
○ 足元では長期金利が上昇し、低金利が懸念点だった 2014年当時とは逆の環境にある。当時の
運用委員会では、金利が上がれば国内債券の期待収益率が高まり、最適化をやり直すことで
国債の割合が上昇し得るとの説明がなされていた。当時の考え方を踏まえれば、現在の基本
ポートフォリオが「金利のある世界」でも最適なのか再考の余地があろう。

### Page 2

2/7 
1. はじめに 
年金積立金管理運用独立行政法人（GPIF）を巡り、基本ポートフォリオの見直し観測が市場で
浮き沈みしている。きっかけは片山さつき財務・金融担当相が2026年7月、GPIFをはじめとする
年金基金について、日本の金融資産への投資をさらに増やす方向を後押ししたいとの考えを示し
たことだ。長期金利が上昇するなかでの発言で、日本の金融資産とは特に国内債券だと解されて
いる。こうしたなか、GPIF側では同年 8月の経営委員会の議事次第に「基本ポートフォリオ検証
等PTにおける議論について」が報告事項に入り、経営委員会への関心が高まった。 
GPIFの基本ポートフォリオは 5年に 1度、公的年金の財政検証を踏まえて見直す。現在の基本
ポートフォリオは2025年度が開始年だ。制度上、毎年度検証し、必要があれば 5年間の中期目標
期間中でも見直しを検討することになっているものの、GPIF発足後、中期目標期間の途中で基本
ポートフォリオを変更したのは 2013年 6月と 2014年 10月の 2回にとどまる。 
2014年10月の見直しでは、国内債券比率を 60％から 35％へ引き下げ、国内外の株式をそれぞ
れ12％から25％へ引き上げる大幅な変更が行われた。株式比率の上昇によって損失が大きくなる
リスクを懸念する向きはあったが、運用損失だけでなく、年金財政上、目標とする利回りを達成
できないこともリスクと捉える考え方を明確にした。2014年改革の本質は公的年金運用における
「安全」という概念を変えたことだと筆者はみている。 
では、仮にいま国内債券を再び増やすとなれば、改革の逆行を意味するのか。この問いを考え
るため、年金財政からの議論と成長戦略からの要請という二つの流れを民主党政権期からたど
り、2014年改革がどのように実現したのかを振り返る。

2. 民主党政権期――国債中心の「安全運用」への疑問 
2014年改革は第2次安倍晋三政権の経済政策「アベノミクス」の象徴の一つとして語られるこ
とが多い。しかし、その成立過程をさかのぼると、改革につながる議論は第 2次安倍政権の発足
前からあった。民主党政権期の議論では、公的年金の運用における「安全」を巡る議論が次第に
成熟していったことが確認できる。 
2006年に発足したGPIFの基本ポートフォリオは、当初国内債券が 67％を占めていた（資料 1）。
このうち、大半を占める国債は満期に額面で償還される。国債偏重の狙いは、厚生労働省の検討
会が 2010年12月にまとめた報告書にも表れている。公的年金運用の考え方として、保険料は
「投資を目的として徴収されたもの」ではないとして、「安全運用が基本」とした（注 1）。検討会
では、全額を国債で運用すべきだとの意見もあった。

### Page 3

3/7 
資料 1 GPIFの基本ポートフォリオの変遷

（注）2014年 10月以降は短期資産を基本ポートフォリオに含めない。 
（出所）GPIFの公表資料より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

もっとも、国債を多く持つことが本当に安全なのかという疑問はすでにこの検討会で示されて
いた。運用上のリスクを単なる損失だけで捉えず、年金財政上予定された収益を下回り、将来の
保険料率の引き上げや給付の引き下げにつながる可能性まで考慮する必要があるとしている。国
債を多く持てば安全だという単純な整理には収まらない議論が存在していたことが確認できる。 
この検討会に続く社会保障審議会年金部会の専門委員会では、国債への集中はリスクになると
いう考えが強く示されるようになる。2012年 2月に同委員会の議論を整理した資料（注2）では
国債のみの運用は一つの資産への集中であり、分散投資ができていないという意味でリスクが非
常に大きい、という指摘が確認できる。デフレと低金利がこの先も続くとは考えにくく、物価や
賃金が上昇すれば、給付する年金額は増える一方で債券価格は下落し、損失を被る可能性が高い
というアベノミクス下での改革を先取りするような意見も出た。国債のみの運用で、将来にわた
り物価や賃金の上昇率を上回る利回りを確保できるのか疑問が残る、との意見もあった。 
民主党政権後期の議論は、短期の運用損失を過度に重視するのではなく、国債偏重によって年
金財政上、必要な利回りを確保できないリスクを考慮する方向へと発展していった。

3. 産業競争力会議――成長戦略からの見直し要請 
GPIF改革に至る過程では、日本経済の成長戦略の文脈で国債偏重の見直しを求める意見があっ
た。これは安倍政権時だけでなく民主党政権期にもあった。国内債券を減らすべきだという方向
は、年金財政からの議論と重なる。ただ、見直しを求める理由は両政権期とも公的年金の巨額の
資金を経済成長に生かすという発想から来ている。 
安倍政権でGPIF改革の舞台となったのは、政権発足直後の2013年 1月に始まった産業競争力会
議だ。「公的・準公的資金の有効活用」が初回から議論にのぼり、安全資産に偏った年金などの運
用を見直し、成長分野への資金供給につなげる考え方が示された。同会議が進むなかでは、民間

### Page 4

4/7 
議員から「アクティブ運用をし、そこに中長期でもの言う株主ということで企業が新陳代謝も進
めるようなツールとしていく」といった発言が飛び出している（注 3）。 GPIFの資金を企業改革や
経済成長に生かそうとする考え方であり、「専ら被保険者の利益」のために運用するという年金積
立金の原則との緊張をはらんでいた。

4. 有識者会議――成長戦略と被保険者利益の両立 
成長戦略のなかで出たこうした要請を、公的年金の運用原則とどのように両立させたのだろう
か。この問題に対応したのが「公的・準公的資金の運用・リスク管理等の高度化等に関する有識
者会議」だった。この会議は政府の日本再興戦略を受けて経済再生担当相の下に設置され、GPIF
改革の理論的柱を打ち立てた。 
公的年金の運用に国民が何を最も期待しているのか明確に定義した点がこの会議のポイントだ
ろう。2013年 11月の報告書は、「現役世代の保険料負担を維持しつつ、将来の年金給付に支障が
生じないよう、年金財政の長期的な健全性の確保に資すること」こそが国民の最も期待している
ことだとした（注4）。目標とする運用利回りが達成できなければ、保険料の引き上げか将来の年
金給付水準の引き下げが迫られる可能性が生じる。 
この定義に則り、デフレから脱却して適度なインフレ環境へ移るというアベノミクスの目指し
た環境でのポートフォリオのあり方を検討した。安全資産とされる国債を多く持てば安全だと考
えるのではなく、年金財政に必要な収益を確保できるかという観点から基本ポートフォリオを考
える方向が明確に示された。 
収益率の向上という文脈のなかで、産業競争力会議で出たようなGPIFの資金を企業改革に生か
すというアイデアは運用改革に取り込まれていく。投資先企業との対話や議決権行使を通じて企
業価値の向上を促し、その成果を運用収益として享受するという考え方が報告書で示された。成
長戦略が期待した企業改革を、被保険者の利益につながる運用につなげたのだ。 
社会保障審議会年金部会の専門委員会もこれに呼応する形で、デフレから脱却して適度なイン
フレ環境へ移る状況では、あらかじめ「国内債券中心」と示す必要はなく、GPIFが運用目的・目
標に沿ったポートフォリオを検討すべきだとした。年金積立金の運用目標は、年金給付費が長期
的には名目賃金上昇率に連動することから、賃金上昇率をどの程度上回るかで設定される。同専
門委員会は、財政検証に向けて検討した複数の経済前提を踏まえ、GPIFが確保すべき運用利回り
として、名目賃金上昇率を 1.7％上回る水準を示した（注5）。

5. GPIF運用委員会――国内債券 35％の決定過程 
ここから2014年 10月の基本ポートフォリオ変更に至る議論を、当時のGPIF運用委員会の議事
録からたどる。2014年改革は、株式を増やした改革とみられがちだ。しかし、運用委員会の議論
の中心になったのは株式の比率ではなく、国内債券の扱いだった。後年公表された議事録を読む
と、国内債券中心では目標の利回りが達成できないどころか、金利が上昇した場合には多額の損
失を被るといった懸念が、当時の事務局や理事長から示されている。当時の長期金利は 0.5％前後

### Page 5

5/7 
と、歴史的な低水準にあった（資料 2）。事務局の試算も、国内債券の比率を 45％以下に減らさな
ければ目標の利回りに届かない、という形で示された。

資料 2 日本国債 10年物利回り

（注）10年物国債利回りの月末値。2026年 11月は 6日の値。 
（出所）Bloombergより第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

GPIF内部の議論では株式をどこまで増やすかではなく、国内債券をどこまで減らすかが議論の
焦点だったことが分かる。運用委員会の委員長は最終段階で、「国債を減らしたというのが一番の
主で、その結果として、こういうポートフォリオが書けた」と振り返っているのが象徴的だろう
（注 6）。 
基本ポートフォリオの決定に至る議論で興味深いのは、GPIFの売買が国内市場に与える影響を
相当程度考慮に入れていた点だ。事務局から示された基本ポートフォリオ候補は当初、五つあっ
た。このなかには、現在のポートフォリオである 4資産を 25％ずつとする案も含まれていた。こ
の案は、現状からの移行を考えると「現実感がゼロ」とされ、早い段階で検証の対象から外れた
（注 7）。事務局は候補を絞り込む際に足元の資産構成からの距離を物差しに加え、最終案の選定
理由にも「足下の資産構成と相対的に近く、移行に係るコストが小さい」ことを挙げている（注
8）。最終段階では、1％刻みで確認すると、経済中位ケースでは国内債券 34％などのより効率的な
配分も見つかった。それでも、国内債券をさらに減らして国内株式を増やせば移行の影響が大き
くなるとして、35％が維持された。外国市場に比べ、国内市場ではGPIFの売買の影響が大きいと
いう判断が働いていた。 
国内債券の売却は、正式な決定を待たずに始まっていた。運用委員会は2014年4月の時点で、
現行の基本ポートフォリオが認める範囲の中で国内債券を減らす方針を了承している。委員長は

### Page 6

6/7 
その目的を、金利上昇に備えて「債券の評価損を抑える」ことだと確認した（注9）。8月には、
その範囲を超えて国内債券を売り、ほかの資産に振り向ける方針も了承された。事務局は、積立
金規模が巨額であるため、市場インパクトやフロントランニングを誘発せずに速やかに資産配分
を変更することは現実的ではないと説明し、基本ポートフォリオの正式決定前から資産配分の変
更を検討する考えを示した（注 10）。正式決定に先立つ 8月末には、国内債券の比率はすでに
50％程度まで下がっていた。 
GPIFの現在の中期目標でも、市場の価格形成や民間の投資行動等を歪めないことが求められて
いる。今後、基本ポートフォリオを変更する場合にも、市場への影響を抑えるため、正式な変更
に先立って資産配分を調整することが考えられる。今回も運用の動向を見極めるうえで、四半期
の運用実績が注目される。

6. おわりに 
2014年にGPIFが中期目標期間の途中で基本ポートフォリオを変更した背景には、先行きの運用
環境の変化を見通した判断があった。GPIFは当時、日本経済が「長年続いたデフレからの転換と
いう大きな運用環境の変化の節目」にあるとし、「長期的な経済環境の変化に速やかに対応する観
点から、来年度を待たず」に変更に踏み切った（注 11）。実際には低金利がその後も長く続いた
が、足元では当時懸念されていた金利上昇が現実のものとなっている。 
長期金利は2026年 9月に 3％台に達し、約 30年ぶりの水準を付けた。奇しくも3％という数字
は、2014年のGPIF運用委員会でもたびたび登場していた。当時の理事長は、国内債券 60％という
従来の基本ポートフォリオについて、「国内債券の利回りが 3％というものを大前提としてつくっ
ている」と述べていた（注 12）。  
興味深いのは、2014年の運用委員会で、金利が上昇すれば国内債券の将来の期待収益率が高ま
り、改めて資産配分の最適化を行うことで「自然に国債の割合がまたもとに戻るというか、上が
っていく」とGPIFの事務局側が説明していたことだ（注 13）。 2020年の基本ポートフォリオ変更
では、国内金利の低下を踏まえ、国内債券を減らして外国債券を増やした。今後は、長期の収益
確保を担う株式の比率を維持しつつ、為替リスクなどを考慮して外国債券の一部を国内債券に振
り向けることも選択肢として考えられる。2014年に国内債券を減らしたのと同じ考え方が、今度
はその割合を引き上げる判断につながり得るのではないだろうか。

金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 11 月 02 日
上週重點訊息
◼ Fed 維持利率不變，兩位委員主張升息：美國聯準會於 10 月 28 日以 10 比 2 的表決結果，
將聯邦基金利率目標區間維持在 3.75%至 4%，其中理事庫克與克里夫蘭聯準銀行總裁哈瑪
克對該決議投下反對票，並主張應升息 1 碼。Fed 在聲明中表示，經濟活動正以強勁步調擴
張，但通膨仍然偏高。在已有兩位委員主張升息的情況下，後續通膨數據將是 Fed 12 月是
否升息的重要影響因素。
◼ Alphabet 自由現金流轉負幅度超出預期：Alphabet 於 10 月 28 日公布第三季財報，自由現

金流為負 90.7 億美元，明顯低於分析師預期的負 27 億美元，同時 Alphabet 維持全年
1,950 億至 2,050 億美元的資本支出指引，顯示 AI 基礎建設投資仍持續消耗公司現金。
Alphabet 該季自由現金流明顯轉負，也引發市場對於科技巨頭資本支出的疑慮，並帶動整體
科技類股出現修正。
◼ 英特爾因 PC 需求疲弱下修第四季財測：英特爾於 10 月 29 日公布第三季財報，營收年增
21%至 165.1 億美元，高於公司原先財測；其中資料中心與 AI 部門營收年增 78%，為 15 年
來最強勁的成長，但以 PC 為主的客戶端運算部門營收則年減 5%。公司指出，由於記憶體短
缺推升 PC 售價，加上企業 IT 預算受限，使 PC 需求轉弱。
市場展望
◼ 科技股短線波動較大，中期看好現金流穩健的 AI 受惠企業：本次財報季顯示雲端營收加速成
長與未認列合約持續擴大，AI 需求仍強；但部分企業的資本支出攀升、自由現金流轉弱，加
上 Fed 可能於 12 月升息，短線科技股波動恐將延續。然而中期而言，AI 產業將由全面成長

轉向基本面分化，具技術優勢、財務結構穩健且 AI 投資已能轉化為獲利的企業，風險報酬相
對有利；對高度依賴外部融資、自由現金流持續為負的企業則宜維持審慎。
◼ 5 至 10 年期美債及投資等級公司債兼顧票息收益與利率風險：美國經濟仍具韌性，加上政府
與企業發債供給維持高檔，可能使市場利率中期仍處於較高水準，但近期公債殖利率回落，
顯示市場避險需求升溫，支撐債券買盤；整體而言，短天期債券面臨未來利率下降後的再投
資風險，長天期債券則較容易受到公債供給增加、期限溢酬及利率波動影響，5-10 年期美國
公債與投資級債券則較能兼顧息收與利率風險。

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國經濟維持穩健擴張，通膨壓力略升溫，Fed 維持利率不變，期中選舉進入
最後衝刺階段 
經濟 
⚫ 美國第三季實質 GDP 季增年率(初值)由上季的 2.2%升至 3.4%，成長主
要受民間消費、企業投資及庫存回補帶動。其中個人消費貢獻 2.52 個百
分點、私人投資貢獻 3.27 個百分點，惟淨出口拖累 3.32 個百分點，整
體經濟仍維持穩健擴張。 
⚫ 9 月美國新屋銷售年化 67.6 萬戶，月減 0.6%、年減 5.3%，主要是中西
部及南部地區銷售下滑拖累整體表現。房價雖較上月回升，但仍低於去
年同期；供給月數 8.4 個月，顯示市場仍呈供給充裕、需求偏弱格局。 
⚫ 美國 9 月個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數年增率由 3.4%升至 3.6%，核心
PCE 物價指數年增率亦由 3.0%升至 3.1%，通膨再度升溫。儘管實質所
得受物價上漲影響而下滑，消費支出仍持續成長，反映家庭需求及內需
動能仍具韌性。 
⚫ 10 月 FOMC 會議以 10 比 2 決議將聯邦基金利率目標區間維持於 3.75%
至 4.0%。決策聲明指出，美國經濟活動表現強勁，就業市場變化不大，
通膨持續高於 2%目標，將依後續數據評估適當政策立場，以確保通膨
回歸目標。兩名委員主張升息 1 碼，顯示內部對通膨風險仍存分歧。主
席 Kevin Warsh 強調將防止能源價格上漲擴散至整體物價，並宣布 2027
年起部分 FOMC 會議改為策略討論會議，以強化長期政策研議。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 10 月 26 日路透社民調顯示，川普施政支持率 32%，略高於前週；國會
選舉政黨支持度則由民主黨 47%領先共和黨 36%。截至 10 月 31 日，
提前及郵寄投票已逾 5,100 萬人。整體而言，期中選舉結果將影響未來
國會版圖、施政推動及貿易政策方向，備受市場關注。 
短評 
⚫ 美國第三季 GDP 成長優於前季，主要受民間消費、企業投資及庫存回
補帶動，顯示內需與企業活動仍具韌性；惟新屋銷售持續疲弱、房市需
求未明顯改善，反映高利率環境仍抑制部分經濟活動。另一方面，PCE
通膨再度升溫，核心通膨降幅有限，顯示物價壓力仍存。在經濟穩健且
通膨高於目標情況下，Fed 維持利率不變並釋出偏鷹訊號，後續貨幣政
策料將維持審慎，持續關注通膨與就業數據變化。

歐洲央行如期暫停升息，12 月升息機率較高 
經濟 ⚫ 歐元區第三季 GDP 季增率為 0.4%。 
重要 
事件 ⚫ 歐洲央行在 10 月 29 日宣布維持政策利率不變。 
短評 
⚫ 第三季受到物價壓力上升、地緣政治緊張局勢等因素影響，對消費及投
資需求構成負面因素，但世足賽以及德國財政支出擴張的支持下，仍使
歐元區經濟保持正向的成長表現。 
⚫ 歐洲央行仍認為近期高漲的能源價格對通膨的壓力相當大，因此，除非
經濟成長受到超出預期的衝擊、能源價格下跌或金融環境急劇收緊，一
般預期歐洲央行在 12 月的政策會議將宣布升息一碼。 
日 
本 
日本央行暫停升息，通膨壓力續存，升息預期升溫 
經濟 
⚫ 9 月工業生產月增 2.2%。 
⚫ 9 月零售月減 0.7%。 
⚫ 10 月東京核心 CPI 年增 2.6%。 
重要 
事件 ⚫ 日本央行維持利率於 1.25%不變，2027 年起加快 ETF 出售速度。 
短評 
⚫ 通膨仍具黏性，在通膨已大致達到 2%的目標、金融環境仍偏寬鬆的情
況下，日本央行貨幣政策正常化方向應不致改變。儘管本次暫緩升息，
但隨通膨風險持續上行，後續仍具升息空間，貨幣政策可望維持漸進緊
縮基調。 
中 
國 
工業部門成本轉嫁能力有限，使利潤率持續萎縮 
經濟 ⚫ 1-9 月工業部門營業收入、利潤年增率分別為 15.5、13.1%。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 中共二十大五中全會，破例將「全面從嚴治黨」列為核心主題，強調反
腐與紀律，沒有推出重大經濟改革方案。 
短評 ⚫ 工業部門營收受到物價上漲帶動，但成本轉嫁能力有限，反而降低利潤 表現。

泰、菲進出口創歷史新高，泰國央行按兵不動以支撐經濟

AI 動能續強，經濟成長動能穩健

增速料將放緩，後續仍須關注 AI 需求延續性、成長動能向傳統產業擴
散情形及金融市場波動等不確定因素。

國際經濟金融週報 
兆豐國際商業銀行 徵信處 
115.11.2 
一、 美國—經濟維持穩健擴張，通膨壓力略升溫，Fed 維持利率不變，期中選
舉進入最後衝刺階段 
2026 年第三季美國實質國內生產毛額(GDP)季增年率初值為 3.4%，高於第二
季之 2.2%，整體景氣持續穩健擴張。就成長貢獻而言，個人消費支出仍為經濟成
長主要支柱，貢獻 2.52 個百分點，其中服務消費及商品消費分別貢獻 1.89 個百
分點及 0.63 個百分點，反映民間需求維持穩健；私人投資則貢獻 3.27 個百分點，
為本季成長最大亮點，其中固定投資貢獻 1.66 個百分點，而企業庫存回補亦額外
貢獻 1.61 個百分點。對外貿易方面，淨出口拖累經濟成長 3.32 個百分點，其中
出口減少造成 0.68 個百分點負面影響；進口增加則拖累 2.64 個百分點。政府消
費支出與投資則受聯邦政府非國防支出增加帶動，貢獻 0.90 個百分點。此外，第
三季個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數及核心 PCE 物價指數分別年增 2.1%及 2.5%，
均低於第二季水準。整體而言，美國第三季經濟成長主要受民間消費、企業投資
及庫存回補支撐，雖貿易逆差擴大對 GDP 形成明顯拖累，但內需表現仍具韌性，
顯示美國經濟基本面維持穩健。 
2026 年 9 月美國新屋銷售經季節性調整後年化戶數為 67.6 萬戶，較上月減
少 0.6%，並較去年同期下降 5.3%，顯示在高利率環境下住宅需求仍較去年疲弱。
地區別表現分歧，其中東北部及西部分別較上月增加 28.0%及 21.4%，惟中西部
及南部分別減少 7.3%及 6.6%，尤其占全美銷售比重最大的南部地區回落，成為
拖累整體銷售的主因。價格方面，9 月新屋銷售中位價為 40.04 萬美元，較上月
上升 3.9%，但較去年同期下跌 3.9%，儘管房價高於上月，惟仍面臨修正壓力。
供給方面，9 月底待售新屋數量為 47.6 萬戶，較上月減少 1.0%，較去年同期減少
2.3%；按目前銷售速度計算，供給月數為 8.4 個月，低於上月的 8.5 個月，但高
於去年同期的 8.2 個月。整體而言，9 月美國新屋銷售較前月小幅下滑且年減幅
度擴大，反映購屋需求仍未明顯改善；雖然部分地區銷售回升且庫存略有下降，
但供給月數仍維持相對偏高水準，顯示新屋市場仍處於供給較充裕、需求偏弱的
格局，後續仍須觀察利率走勢及就業市場變化對住宅需求的影響。 
2026 年 9 月美國個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數月增 0.5%，高於 8 月之 0.3%，

年增率則由 3.4%升至 3.6%；扣除食品及能源後之核心 PCE 月增率維持於 0.3%，
年增率由 3.0%微升至 3.1%，能源價格回升及服務價格上漲持續對整體物價形成
支撐，通膨再度升溫。另一方面，個人所得月增 0.2%，可支配所得(DPI)亦月增
0.2%，個人消費支出則月增 0.5%，增幅高於所得成長，顯示家庭消費需求仍具韌
性；惟受物價上漲影響，實質可支配所得月減 0.3%，實質個人消費支出則持平，
反映名目支出增加部分已被物價上升所抵銷。整體而言，美國 9 月消費活動維持
穩健，內需仍為經濟成長提供支撐，但通膨壓力較前月略有升溫，物價回落至聯
準會 2%目標之過程仍具挑戰，後續仍須持續關注能源價格變化、核心通膨走勢
及消費動能對聯準會貨幣政策方向之影響。 
聯準會(Fed)於 2026 年 10 月 FOMC 會議以 10 比 2 表決結果，決定將聯邦基
金利率目標區間維持於 3.75%至 4.00%不變，符合市場預期；謹將本次會議聲明
及主席 Kevin Warsh 記者會重點摘錄如下：(一)聯準會認為美國經濟活動持續以
強勁步調擴張，較前次聲明所稱之「穩健擴張」進一步上修對經濟表現之評價；
儘管地緣政治情勢使經濟前景不確定性仍偏高，但國內支出維持韌性，生產力成
長與資本投資表現強勁，就業增幅大致與勞動力成長同步，失業率變動不大，顯
示整體經濟基本面仍具相當韌性；(二)通膨仍高於聯準會 2%目標，委員會表示需
累積更多經濟與物價資訊，以評估適當政策立場，確保通膨適時回歸 2%目標，
並重申將致力維持物價穩定；(三)本次決議出現兩張反對票，聯準會理事 Lisa Cook
及克里夫蘭聯邦準備銀行總裁 Beth Hammack 主張升息 1 碼，反映部分委員對通
膨風險仍抱持較為謹慎態度；(四)主席 Kevin Warsh 於記者會表示，近期聯邦公
開市場委員會討論焦點多集中於物價穩定，特別是在近期能源及相關價格再度上
揚情況下，聯準會有責任避免個別產業價格上漲擴散至整體經濟，並確保通膨預
期維持穩定；(五)Warsh 並宣布調整 FOMC 會議安排，2027 年仍將維持每年 8 次
會議，但其中 1 次將改為專門討論貨幣政策策略之會議，不進行利率表決或發布
政策聲明，並預期 2028 年將有 2 次會議採取此模式，以騰出更多時間進行長期
政策與重大議題研議。整體而言，本次會議雖維持利率不變，但聯準會仍認為通
膨問題尚未完全解決，後續政策方向仍將取決於通膨、勞動市場及整體經濟數據
表現。 
根據 10 月 26 日路透社最新民調，總統川普施政支持率為 32%，較前一週之
31%略為回升；在國會選舉政黨支持度方面，47%的登記選民表示傾向支持民主

支持度上仍具優勢，但相關結果僅反映調查期間之民意分布，尚難直接推論最終
選舉結果。另一方面，根據 10 月 31 日統計，已有超過 5,100 萬名選民透過提前
投票或郵寄投票完成投票，投票規模可望創下近年新高。選戰最後階段，通膨與

州的選務程序、郵寄選票資格認定及選舉公正性相關爭議亦持續受到輿論關注。
整體而言，本次期中選舉結果除將影響國會兩院席次分布外，亦可能牽動川普政
府後續施政推動、預算協商及貿易政策方向，受到國際市場高度關注。 
二、歐元區—歐洲央行如期暫停升息，12 月升息機率較高 
 歐元區第三季 GDP 季增率為 0.4%，但有部份是受到愛爾蘭經濟成長率大幅
波動的扭曲，若剔除愛爾蘭，則歐元區第三季 GDP 季增率僅約 0.2%。該季受到
物價壓力上升、地緣政治緊張局勢等因素影響，對消費及投資需求構成負面因素，
但世足賽以及德國財政支出擴張的支持下，仍使歐元區經濟保持正向表現。就國
別而言，德、法、義、西 GDP 季增率分別為 1.2%、-0.1%、0.8%、2.7%；其中德
國經濟活動受到財政擴張的提振；法國的消費支出雖強勁，但受到投資減緩和進
口增加拖累；義大利製造業產出萎縮拖累成長表現；西班牙在住宅營建熱潮的支
持下，經濟活動持續優於其他國家，但歐盟 NGEU 計畫到期以及政府財政整頓力
道，西班牙後續經濟動能可能會逐漸放緩。 
 歐洲央行在 10 月 29 日宣布維持政策利率不變，主要是近期歐元區各國殖利
率普遍上升，對經濟造成限制性因素，且不確定性可能會隨著時間的推移而累積，
從而拖累經濟成長。不過，歐洲央行仍認為近期高漲的能源價格對通膨的壓力相
當大，使央行考慮進一步採取貨幣政策措施因應。因此，除非經濟成長受到超出
預期的衝擊、能源價格下跌或金融環境急劇收緊，一般預期歐洲央行在 12 月的
政策會議將會宣布升息一碼。 
三、日本—日本央行暫停升息，通膨壓力續存，升息預期升溫 
 9 月工業生產月增 2.2%，扭轉 8 月受天災造成生產中斷而下滑的情勢，從細
項來看，汽車、電子產品及生產機械產量均呈回升，其中生產機械已連續第四個
月成長，反映 AI 相關資本支出需求持續暢旺；庫存月增 2.5%，石化產品庫存亦
逐步回補。展望未來，製造商預估 10 月及 11 月產量將分別月增 5.1%及 0.8%，

顯示在 AI 及資本支出需求、汽車復產與庫存回補支撐下，短期工業生產動能可
望維持強勁。 
 9 月零售月減 0.7%，主要受雨季抑制家庭外出消費，以及先前受新規上路前
換機需求及汽車稅減免政策帶動的耐久財消費動能逐漸減弱，致整體消費動能偏
弱；惟服裝及一般商品銷售受假期延長與節慶需求支撐而有所回升。另 10 月消
費者信心指數下降 0.4 點至 35.0，連續第二個月下滑，反映消費者對物價上漲及
實質購買力下降的憂慮加深，預期未來一年通膨率超過 2%的消費者比重亦升至
79.8%。展望未來，隨天候等暫時性不利因素逐漸消退，加上所得成長仍具韌性，
第四季消費動能可望回升；惟通膨持續升溫，加上汽油補貼逐步退場，恐進一步
侵蝕購買力，為消費復甦的下行風險。 
 10 月東京核心 CPI 年增 2.6%，石油相關成本上漲已由核心商品逐步擴散至服
務價格，食品及生鮮食品價格亦偏強。雖目前能源、汽油補貼等政府措施仍抑制
通膨，但隨 12 月相關補貼逐步退場，成本壓力將進一步反映於商品及服務價格，
預期至 2026 年底潛在通膨仍可能高於央行的 2%目標，通膨黏性值得持續關注。 
 日本央行於 10 月 29、30 日召開貨幣政策會議，維持政策利率於 1.25%不變，
主要係仍需觀察先前升息的累積效果，但整體政策訊號仍偏鷹派。本次決議以 8：
1 通過，唯一反對的政策委員高田創主張再升息 1 碼至 1.5%，反映央行內部對通
膨上行風險仍具警戒。日本央行指出通膨已大致達到與 2%通膨目標相符的水準，
同時將 2027 財年的核心 CPI(不含生鮮食品)預測由 2.4%上調至 2.6%，顯示目前
通膨情勢仍支持貨幣政策進一步正常化。央行總裁表示，目前金融環境仍屬寬鬆，
未來若經濟及物價走勢符合預期，仍將適時調升政策利率，市場因而持續關注後
續會議再度升息的可能性。此外，日本央行亦決定自 2027 年 1 月起，將 ETF 出
售規模由約 3,300 億日圓提高至 8,600 億日圓，加速資產負債表正常化進程。 
四、中國—工業部門成本轉嫁能力有限，使利潤率持續萎縮 
1-9 月工業部門營業收入、利潤年增率分別為 15.5、13.1%，其中營業收入年
增率較上期(1-8 月)大增 8.9 個百分點，但利潤年增率下降 4.5 個百分點。受惠於
金屬、塑化原物料上漲，加上 AI 需求擴張，以及政府推動高科技產業發展等因
素，營收成長貢獻度較高者包含有色金屬加工(營收、利潤年增 17.5%、79.7%)、
計算機、通信和其他電子設備製造業(營收、利潤年增 21%、103.8%)、化學原料

五、印度及東南亞—泰、菲進出口創歷史新高，泰國央行按兵不動以支撐經濟 
(1) 新加坡：9 月消費者物價指數(CPI)年增率小幅回落至 2.2%、核心通膨率自 8
月的 2.2%降至 2%，反映交通運輸（7.4%）、服裝鞋帽（2.1%）及家庭耐用品
和服務（0.2%）價格漲幅顯著放緩，此外，由於行業競爭壓力，資訊通信技術
服務價格的進一步回落也抑制整體服務價格的上漲。隨著 11 月電力和天然氣
價格下調生效，預計 2026 年第四季核心通膨將進一步放緩。 
(2) 泰國：9 月出口額達 384.4 億美元、年增 24.0%，創歷史新高，主要得益於工
業產品出口成長 25.4%，其中，以電腦及相關設備（59.4%）、電話及零件
（117.5%）及電路板（45.9%）成長最為顯著，對美國出口額年增 50.1%，對
中國和日本的出口額分別成長 18.4%和 19.0%。9 月進口額 443 億美元、年增
50.4%，創歷史新高，因出口導向製造業生產需求及全球能源價格的上漲，主
要類別進口額均有所成長，其中，資本貨物(電腦、機械零件、電路板等)進口
大增 92.3%，燃料、原材料和中間產品進口增幅均逾 4 成、運輸設備在飛機與
航空設備進口大增 12 倍下成長 24%。9 月貿易逆差大幅擴大至 59 億美元，累
計 1 至 9 月，貿易逆差達 437 億美元，出口額成長 19.6%、進口額成長 37.8%。
泰國央行 10 月會議維持基準利率於 1%不變，維持寬鬆的貨幣政策立場，以
支持經濟復甦的不平衡。經濟預估方面，強於預期的出口以及與科技和人工智
慧週期相關的投資，預估 2026 年和 2027 年經濟成長率將分別成長 2.4%和
2.1%，然而，高額的家庭債務、脆弱的勞動市場和疲軟的購買力繼續抑制私人
消費，使經濟成長仍低於潛在水平。通膨方面，受能源價格上漲和聖嬰現象的
影響，預計通膨率在 2027 年初將出現短暫上升，之後隨著國內需求的疲軟而
回落，2026 年和 2027 年整體通膨率平均分別為 2.1%和 1.3%。

六、台灣—AI 動能續強，經濟成長動能穩健 
9 月景氣對策信號續呈紅燈，綜合判斷分數為 41 分，較上月增加 1 分，燈號
續呈紅燈，其中工業及服務業加班工時，受惠 AI 相關產業生產動能維持熱絡，
由綠燈轉呈黃紅燈，其餘八項燈號均維持不變。另領先與同時指標皆呈上升，顯
示國內經濟動能維持穩健。 
主計總處 10 月 30 日公布台灣第三季經濟成長率為 14.36%，主要受 AI 需求
強勁帶動出口及企業投資大幅成長，民間消費亦維持熱絡，顯示外需動能正逐步
向內需擴散，整體經濟呈現內外皆熱。展望未來，AI 需求、半導體相關投資及企
業資本支出持續擴張，加上年底消費旺季，可望支撐短期景氣表現；惟今年經濟
高速成長墊高比較基期，明年增速料將放緩，後續仍須關注 AI 需求延續性、成
長動能向傳統產業擴散情形及金融市場波動等不確定因素。

### Page 1

Economic Trends

2026 年 11 月 6 日

1/2 
2026 年・冬のボーナス予測 
～前年比＋3.0％と６年連続の増加を予想～ 
 経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト  新家 義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

○ 民間企業の2026 年冬のボーナス一人当たり支給額を前年比＋3.0％（昨冬同＋2.8％）と予想する
（厚生労働省「毎月勤労統計」ベース）。冬のボーナスとしては６年連続の増加となるだろう。中
東情勢の悪化に伴う資源価格上昇が企業活動の重荷となるなかでも企業収益は高水準を維持してい
る。人手不足を背景とした処遇改善の動きも根強く、今冬もボーナスの増加が続くと予想する。

○ 11 月６日に厚生労働省から公表された26 年夏のボーナス（賞与支給事業所における労働者一人平
均賞与額）は、前年比＋5.3％と５年連続で増加し、昨夏の同＋2.9％から伸びも拡大した。また、
賞与を支給していない事業所も含む「全事業所における労働者一人平均賞与額」で見ても同＋
5.9％と増加している。賞与を支給する事業所の割合も前年の 73.4％から74.0％へ上昇しており、
企業の従業員への還元姿勢が強いことがうかがえる。

○ ボーナスの交渉では、春闘時にその年の年間賞与を決定する夏冬型が採用されていることが多い。
2026 年春闘では、月例給与だけでなく一時金でも増額回答が目立った。企業収益が高水準にある
ことに加え、物価高への配慮や人材確保の観点から、賞与を含めた処遇改善を進める企業が多かっ
たとみられる。また、月例給与の引き上げが賞与の算定基礎額を押し上げ、支給月数の増加と相ま
ってボーナス増につながる面もある。夏のボーナス増に続き、冬についてもこうした交渉結果が反
映される形で増加が予想される。

○ 毎期型の企業、あるいは労働組合が存在しない中小・零細企業においては、より直近の収益状況・
業況がボーナスに反映されやすい。もっとも、イラン情勢悪化によるコスト上昇が企業収益の下押
し要因となるなかでも、価格転嫁の進展などを背景として企業収益は総じて底堅く推移している。
利益の水準も高く、従業員への還元余力はなお保たれているとみてよいだろう。

○ 人手不足感が強いことも、ボーナス増加を後押しする。企業にとって人材の確保・定着は引き続き
重要な経営課題となっており、月例給与だけでなく賞与を含めた処遇改善の必要性は高い。物価高
によって家計負担が増加するなか、従業員への還元を求める声も強い。こうした点も今冬のボーナ
ス増加を支える要因となる。

○ 労務行政研究所が９月30 日に公表した調査によれば、東証プライム上場企業 175 社の 2026 年年末

### Page 2

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
2/2 
一時金妥結額は、前年比＋4.2％と５年連続で増加した。製造業が同＋4.3％、非製造業が同＋
3.5％と、いずれもプラスとなっている。大企業中心の調査であり、中小企業やパートタイム労働
者も含む毎月勤労統計とは対象が異なるものの、冬のボーナスも堅調に増加するという見方を裏付
ける結果といえる。

○ 冬のボーナス増加は家計の所得環境にとって好材料であり、個人消費を下支えすることが期待され
る。一方で懸念されるのが物価上昇の影響である。中東情勢悪化に伴うコスト上昇の価格転嫁など
を背景として、今後は物価上昇率が高まる可能性が高く、政府による電気・ガス代支援の効果を織
り込んでも、2027 年初にはＣＰＩコアの前年比上昇率が＋３％台に達することが予想される。物
価上昇による家計の購買力の圧迫が続くなか、ボーナス増加による個人消費の押し上げ効果につい
ては過度な期待は禁物だろう。

（出所）厚生労働省「毎月勤労統計」
（注）26年冬の予測値は第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所

MPI Corporation  | Asia Pacific
Potential further upside in
2027/28 capacity expansion;

MPI hosted its 3Q26 earnings call on November 16. Key takeaways:
There could be further upside in 2027/28 capacity expansion: The company
reiterated that it would add at least 2.5mn pins for MEMS and 0.5mn pins for VPC in
2027. It may raise the numbers after its budget plan is finalized in December. MPI
expects to maintain aggressive capacity expansion in 2028, given strong demand.

2027 guidance appears conservative: The company guided for 2027 revenue to be
up at least 50% YoY with gross margin >60% –   conservative vs. consensus
expectations of 2027 revenue doubling. However, we expect MPI to beat its own
guidance  and raise it gradually, per previous practice of amendment.
In qualification for second insertion 2 customer: The company is  still in
qualification for insertion 2. However, it confirmed it has a second significant

customer; the schedule of the qualification could be similar to the first one. MPI said
it would be the only supplier for the first  customer's  insertion 2 process. CPO could
make up a double-digit share of 2028 total revenue.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
                        MPI Corporation (6223.TWO)                    
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture 
the long-term value. We assume a 9.78% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium 
6.0%), a payout ratio of 67%, a medium-term growth rate of 16.0%, and a terminal growth rate 
of 4.0%. Our price target implies 50x/25x 2027e/2028e P/E. We believe its valuation will con-
tinue to climb along with growing earnings.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113)金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報

新代(7750)：中國高階控制器需求持續強勁 
建議：評等買進

一、新代為台灣精密設備控制器
大廠 
 新代成立於 1995 年，為專注於精密設備控制器之大廠，以自有品牌 SYNTEC
行銷全球。主營產品涵蓋 CNC 數控系統、伺服產品與產業專機；集團亦積極佈
局新成長動能，旗下「聯達智能」與「正鉑雷射」分別專攻機器人/雲端智造
（IoT/MES）及工業減碳製程設備。公司具備兩大核心優勢：其一為精準的市場
定位，產品品質媲美德日大廠（如 Siemens、Fanuc），同時具備極佳的性價比，
成功填補高階進口與中低階國產間的市場缺口；其二為軟硬整合與在地服務，透
過橫跨歐美、亞太及中國 24 個分公司的全球據點，提供即時服務以協助機械廠
進行差異化升級。目前新代服務逾 2,000 家客戶，單一客戶營收占比均低於 5%，
客戶結構極具分散性與營運韌性，築起穩固的長期競爭護城河。

二、產品組合優化抵銷原物料成
本上漲，3Q26 毛利維持約 44%
水準 
 受惠中國工具機及金屬加工產業需求強勁、終端客戶持續升級高階控制器，以及
競爭對手關鍵零組件供應受限帶動轉單，新代 3Q26 營收達 78.05 億元，QoQ-
5.5%、YoY+157.7%，雖受傳統淡季影響較上季下滑，但仍創歷史單季次高。獲
利方面， 儘管記憶體價格上漲，受惠高階控制器銷售比重提升及產品組合優化，
3Q26 毛利率達 44.44%，季增 0.35 個百分點、年減 0.04 個百分點，維持公司
長期指引 45%±2%區間； 隨營收規模擴大及費用率改善，3Q26 營業利益達
21.06 億元，QoQ-11.9%、YoY+398.2%，營業利益率達 26.98%，年增 13.02
個百分點；稅後淨利達 16.80 億元，QoQ-11.4%、YoY+360.2%，單季 EPS 達
24.06 元，創歷史單季次高。1-3Q26 稅後淨利達 46.50 億元，YoY+221.7%，
EPS 達 66.59 元，反映高階產品需求成長、轉單效益及營運規模擴張持續挹注
獲利。

三、中國高階控制器滲透率持續
提升，海外布局與產能擴張挹注
2027 年成長動能 
 2026 年中國工具機產業維持成長動能，1–8 月金屬切削相關產業成長約 18–
19%，受惠製造業升級、五軸加工及車銑複合機等高階設備需求增加，帶動控制
器與伺服系統銷售。新代憑藉高階產品性能、較國際競品約 20%的價格優勢，
以及中國逾 2,000 家活躍客戶與在地直銷服務網絡，持續擴大市占率，2026 年
控制器出貨量預估 YoY+70–80%，並受惠日系競爭對手關鍵零組件供應受限，
取得大型工具機客戶轉單。儘管部分日系廠商供貨逐步恢復，公司透過提前備貨
及與記憶體原廠簽訂供應協議，預期關鍵零組件供應可維持至 2027 年下半年，
搭配產品組合優化及客戶黏著度提升，有望延續競爭優勢。海外布局方面，印度
市場受惠製造業在地化政策及工具機產業升級需求，帶動高階控制器國產替代
與供應商多元化商機。公司已與印度前十大工具機廠建立合作接洽，並取得兩筆
單筆超過 100 套的訂單，預期 2026 年印度營收 YoY+100%，惟目前營收貢獻

仍低；中長期目標五年內印度市占率提升至 10%，並持續擴大東南亞市場布局。
產能方面，蘇州二廠預計於 2027 年農曆年前後完成搬遷，馬來西亞新廠預計
1Q27 完工，隨新產能陸續投入，預計 1H27 整體產能將擴充至目前約 2.6 倍，
另有機械手臂產能增加，為後續營收成長提供支撐。展望 2027 年，管理層預期
中國工具機產業成長約 10–15%，公司目標營收成長 25–30%，並持續推動新代
控制器、聯達機械手臂及正鉑智慧工廠整合方案，搭配海外市場拓展及 AI 技術
導入，帶動營收與獲利持續成長。

四、評等買進理由  綜合前述，展望 2027 年，新代受惠中國高階工具機需求成長、日系競爭對手轉
單效益延續，以及控制器市占率持續提升，營運成長動能可望延續。隨蘇州二廠
及馬來西亞新廠陸續投產，預計自 2Q27 起新增產能逐步開出，整體控制器產能
有望擴充至目前約 2.5 倍，搭配印度大型工具機客戶導入加速，以及多合一高階
控制器與智慧自動化產品持續推出，有助推升產品 ASP 及獲利能力，預估 2026
年稅 EPS 為 91.49，預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 為 118.49。至於評價面部分，過往
3 年 PER 平均落在 25x-35x，目前約 16x，處歷史區間下緣，中國市占率提升、
印度市場拓展、新產能貢獻及智慧製造業務成長等中長期動能，認為目前評價具
吸引力，故給予買進評等，目標價 2300 元(25x2026EPS)。

주가 추이

3Q26 매출액과 영업이익은 모두 당사 추정치를 크게 하회했습니다. 탁월
한 차량 판매 증가세에도 불구하고 부정적 믹스와 환율/원자재가 상승에 따
른 수익성 악화가 아쉬웠습니다. 매크로 영향 고려한 추정치 하향 및 로봇 
지분가치 할인으로 목표주가 21만원으로 조정합니다. 
큰 폭의 판매 성장에도 수익성 크게 악화 
동사 3Q26 매출액은 31.6조원(+10.1% 이하 YoY), 영업이익 1.8조원
(+24.3%)을 기록하며 추정치를 크게 하회했다. 관세 영향이 본격화되었
던 전년 동기 대비 관세율 차이에 따른 비용 감소(5.3천억원)와 판매보증 
충당부채 전입액 감소(6.7천억원) 등 낮은 기저로 이익이 개선되었으나, 
2023년 생산 정상화 이후 가장 큰 폭의 판매 성장(84.7만대, +7.9%)에도 
불구하고 인센티브와 재료비 상승, 중동 물량 감소와 경제형 EV 판매 확
대에 따른 부정적 믹스로 OPM 5.8%에 그친 실적을 기록했다.  
예상보다 큰 부정적 PT/지역 믹스 영향 
기대를 상회하는 판매량 증가에 따라 물량 효과 통한 수익성 개선을 기
대했으나, 예상 대비 큰 3Q 부정적 믹스 영향에 따라 4Q와 ’27년 실적 
개선 기대 또한 낮출 수밖에 없는 상황이다. 아중동 판매 부진과 유럽 모
델 수익성 저하 영향이 판매 물량 증가 효과보다 크게 작용하고 있다. 인
도, 중남미 대비 고마진 차종 믹스가 좋은 중동의 지정학적 영향이 지속
되고 있고, 유럽은 EV2 등 신차 중심 판매 확대에도 중국 로컬업체들의 
유럽 공세 격화로 시장 점유율을 사수하기 위한 모델 저수익화가 예상 
대비 장기화될 것으로 판단한다. 그러나 고정비 만회를 위해 인센티브 
증가와 가격 효과 축소 영향 속에서도 판매 확대는 불가피한 상황이다. 
부정적 상황 장기화 가능성, 목표주가 210,000원으로 하향 
결국, 수익성 개선을 위해서는 고마진 제품 판매 확대 또는 판매가 견조
하나 저마진 차종 원가를 절감하는 방법밖에 없다. 북미 텔루라이드 
HEV 판매와 스포티지HEV HMGMA 증산이 고마진 물량 증가에 지속
기여할 것이다. 그러나 단기 중동 시장 회복과 차종별 원가절감 효과 확
인이 어렵다는 점에서 고마진 제품 판매 확대 효과는 제한적 전망이다. 
이에, 투자의견 Buy 유지하되, 목표주가 21만원으로 조정한다. 실적 추
정치 조정하여 영업가치를 종전 대비 15.7% 하향하고, 성장주 밸류 디스
카운트에 따른 로봇 지분가치 30% 할인하였다.

자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 7

기아 (000270) [한화리서치]

[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

Document 272: Anthropic开盘大涨，一切看似完美！随后风云突变，美股跳水，发生了什么？

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic开盘大涨，一切看似完美！随后风云突变，美股跳水，发生了什么？.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

周四， Anthropic 史上最⼤规模 IPO 登陆市场，开盘⼀度⼤涨逾 38% ， AI 交易看似迎来强⼒
背书。然⽽，这场盛宴来得快，去得也快 —— 美股全天⾼开低⾛，标普 500 收跌 2% ，纳指
跌 2.25% 。做空机构突袭 AMD 、逾千亿 IPO 抽⼲流动性、负 Gamma 仓位创五年极值 ……AI
盛宴变成了美股压⼒测试。
摘要：
11 ⽉ 19 ⽇， Anthropic 以 83 美元定价上市，开盘即飙升⾄ 111.50 美元，全场沸腾。但 Anthropic 开
盘即是⾼点，股价随即⼀路回落，收盘 97 美元，涨幅收窄⾄ 17% 。
与此同时，美股⼤盘也是冲⾼回落的⾛势 —— 纳斯达克从开盘涨逾 2% ，到收盘跌 2.25% ，单⽇
振幅超过 4 个百分点。标普 500 当⽇蒸发市值约 1.3 万亿美元，与 Anthropic 此次 IPO 募资规模⾼度
吻合。
美股盘中，做空机构 Hunterbrook 发布报告做空 AMD ，拖累 AI 板块整体承压；逾千亿美元的 IPO

融资规模抽⾛市场流动性，叠加做市商处于极端负 gamma 状态及⽉度期权到期压⼒，成为市场
剧烈波动的核⼼推⼿。 Anthropic 上市⾸⽇的剧情，本该是 AI 信仰的加固时刻，但却意外演变成
⼀场压⼒测试。
开盘：⼀切看起来很美好
11 ⽉ 19 ⽇周四美股开盘前，市场弥漫着久违的乐观⽓氛。
Anthropic 以 83 美元的发⾏价挂牌，⾼于此前路演区间上限，对应估值约 1.1 万亿美元，创下 IPO
史上最⾼募资规模，消息提振隔夜美股期货全线⾛⾼。
美东时间早盘 9 时 30 分，纳斯达克综合指数随即冲⾼逾 2% ，标普 500 同步上涨，市场情绪⼀度⾼
涨。彼时，市场普遍将这笔史上最⼤规模的科技 IPO 解读为 AI 交易依然健康的有⼒信号。
Direxion 资本市场主管 Jake Behan 表⽰： “ 此前市场⼀直在讨论 AI 交易是否已经⾛过了头，所以
先看到英伟达超预期，再看到交易员愿意追逐 Anthropic ⾼于发⾏价的价格，这是相当值得关注
的信号。”“ 这并不能终结所有估值争议，但它有⼒地表明，市场还没准备好放弃这笔交易。 ”

⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

然⽽，好景不⻓。
开盘即⾼点
就在开盘后不久，⼀条彭博等媒体报道的消息迅速在华尔街交易台蔓延：“Anthropic IPO 据报
⼏乎未向⼤多数机构买家配售。 ”
这条消息让市场瞬间警觉。 ZeroHedge 分析指出，这背后有两种解读：
要么是承销商对需求极度⾃信，刻意压缩机构配额以确保上市后股价爆发；
要么，是机构根本不愿以 83 美元的价格接盘，最终只能将筹码塞给对价格更不敏感的散⼾。
市场似乎倾向于后者。
上午⾄午间： AMD 暴跌，芯⽚板块遭殃
就在⼤盘摇摇欲坠之际，另⼀颗 “ 炸弹 ” 引爆。
做空机构 Hunterbrook 发布报告，披露其已做空 AMD，并指控该公司持续向被美国列⼊⿊名单
的企业提供芯⽚固件更新服务，同时与此前引发英伟达监管争议的部分企业存在关联。
AMD 随即回应： “ 这些说法歪曲了 AMD 的业务实践和合作关系。 ”
但声明并未能⽌住跌势。 AMD 单⽇跌幅超 9% ，拖累整个芯⽚及 AI 板块集体⾛低。
正午时分， Anthropic 开盘即遭抛售

美东时间正午 12 时， Anthropic 正式开盘，开盘价 111.50 美元，较 83 美元发⾏价⼤涨逾 34% 。
然⽽，就在开盘的瞬间，股价便遭到抛售 ——“ 利好兑现 ” 的逻辑应声⽽⾄。与此同时，⼤盘也⼏
乎在同⼀时刻跌⾄⽇内低点。
据《纽约时报》报道，这⼀⾛势与 SpaceX 今年 6 ⽉上市时如出⼀辙，彼时同样出现了开盘冲⾼
后快速回落的⾏情，投资者对此早有⼼理准备。
Anthropic 最终以每股 97 美元收盘，涨幅收窄⾄ 17% 。

### Page 3

下午：跌势加速，全线溃退
美东午后，跌势进⼀步蔓延。
纳指从开盘涨逾 2% ，⼀路反转⾄收跌 2.25% ，单⽇振幅超 4 个百分点。标普 500 收跌 2% ， 11 个
板块全线飘绿，市值单⽇蒸发约 1.3 万亿美元 —— 与 Anthropic 上市时约 1.3 万亿美元的估值规模
⼏乎相当。
这场抛售，不只是科技股的问题。标普 500 消费可选板块所有成分股在午后均告下跌，领跌的是
Williams-Sonoma—— 该公司公布的第三季度业绩显⽰，销售额未达分析师预期。强劲的消费⽀
出⼀直是今年美国经济韧性的重要⽀柱，投资者对消费放缓的信号格外敏感。
当⽇美股 VIX 恐慌指数升⾄近 28 ， VVIX （波动率的波动率）突破 125 ，标普 500 ⼀个⽉隐含相关
性同步攀升 —— 分析称，这些指标均指向⼀个⼴泛的去⻛险环境，⽽⾮个股层⾯的孤⽴性抛
售。
这也是标普 500 连续第六个交易⽇下跌，为 2024 年 4 ⽉以来最⻓连跌纪录。

### Page 4

幕后推⼿还有负 Gamma 与流动性抽离
这场暴跌，还有多条隐藏的线索。
第⼀，技术结构极度脆弱。
野村证券策略师 Charlie McElligott 此前已预警，在英伟达财报、 Anthropic 上市与期权到期 “ 四重
⻛险 ” 叠加之下，做市商的 “ 负 Gamma” 敞⼝已升⾄ 2019 年以来最⾼⽔平。
当做市商处于负 Gamma 状态时，他们被迫顺势对冲 —— 市场下跌时卖出，进⼀步放⼤跌幅。
0DTE （零⽇到期）期权在下午的流量⼤量涌⼊，进⼀步压垮了较⻓期限的⽀撑。
第⼆， IPO 本⾝ “ 抽⼲ ” 了市场流动性。
这笔 IPO 合计募资 813 亿美元（含 267 亿美元的 vendor placement ，总计逾 1000 亿美元）。交易台
消息称，市场参与者为筹集认购资⾦，抛售了⻩⾦、铜、加密货币以及英伟达等 AI 代理资产。这
⼀说法与盘⾯吻合：⻩⾦跌破 3800 美元、铜价⼤跌约 5% 、加密货币全线回调。
第三，消费板块拖累更为明显。
标普 500 所有⾮必需消费品股票在午后均告下跌。强劲的消费⽀出⼀直是今年美国经济韧性的核

⼼⽀柱，这⼀信号令投资者对消费放缓的担忧升温。
第四，年末获利了结压⼒。
与此同时，机构投资者也有⾃⼰的盘算。《纽约时报》指出，尽管近期市场波动加剧，投资者今
年整体仍享有丰厚回报。感恩节前后，交易员倾向于锁定利润，因为年末市场流动性趋于收
缩，届时以理想价格成交的难度会更⼤。
乐观者还未放弃
⾼盛分析师 Rich Privorotsky 在当天早间发出预警， “ 今天感觉很重要 ” 。他指出，机构对科技股
的净仓位约为 18% ，远低于指数 29% 的权重，处于五年极值附近的低配状态 —— 这意味着只要
市场不⼤幅下跌，就可能触发补仓式轧空⾏情。
Direxion 也表⽰，市场对 Anthropic IPO 整体上仍持积极态度，英伟达超预期财报与 Anthropic 的
顺利上市，共同提供了 “AI 交易尚未终结 ” 的信⼼背书。

### Page 5

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

Topics  Regulatory reform in India's insurance sector

Over the past quarter century, the Indian government has pursued the liberalization of the country's insurance market, 
with the aim of bringing stability to people's living standards and strengthening financial intermediation functions. 
Recently, however, it has begun to reverse course, tightening rules in order to better protect consumers.

■ India's insurance market has grown rapidly with deregulation 
The Indian government has long pursued 
the development of the country's insurance 
market, with the aim of bringing stability to 
people's living standards and strengthening 
financial intermediation functions. This 
stance can be attributed to two background 
factors:  
The first is that India's social security 
system remains underdeveloped. As a result, 
enrollment in health and life insurance can 
provide a vital safety net for mitigating the 
risk of sharp declines in household income 
due to unexpected illnesses or accidents.  
The second is that the sustainable 
development of the Indian economy requires 
the accumulation of long-term and stable 
domestic capital, separate from short-term 
bank deposits, in order to finance the 
development of infrastructure and the 
nurturing of industry. The hope has been that 
the enormous sums of money collected and 
managed by insurance companies can serve 
as a reliable source for this long-term 
financing.  
These factors have led the Indian 
government to walk a path of gradual 
deregulation over the past quarter century. 
The process began with the liberalization of 
market entry by private and foreign 
companies in 2000, which was followed by 
a lifting of the ban on "bancassurance" (the 
sale of insurance through bank branches) in 
2002, the raising of the ceiling on foreign 
ownership, and other reforms.  
This series of deregulatory measures 
spurred the entry of numerous private and foreign 
firms, causing market competition to intensify. Several 
Japanese insurers, including Nippon Life and the Tokio 
Marine Group, also entered the market, helping to drive 
investment in India.  
Besides deregulation related to market entry and 
sales, other factors also contributed to the rapid growth 
of India's insurance market, namely the expansion of 
the middle class, improvements in household financial 
literacy, and the digitalization of insurance services. 
Premium income grew at a pace exceeding economic 
growth, and in recent years insurance companies' assets 
under management have reached a level equivalent to 
Year Event
Establishment of the IRDAI (Insurance Regulatory
and Development Authority of India)
Acceptance of market entry applications from
domestic private firms and foreign entities
(foreign equity cap set at 26%)
2002
Authorization of bancassurance (sale of insurance
through banks or bank-owned distribution
channels)
2015 Increase in foreign equity cap from 26% to 49%
2016
Introduction of a post-facto notification system
for the sale of new insurance products (replacing
the previous prior-approval system)
2021 Increase in foreign equity cap from 49% to 74%
2022 Rationalization of regulations regarding online and diverse insurance distribution channels
2023
Removal of caps on commissions paid by
insurance companies to agents and
intermediaries
2025 Increase in foreign equity cap from 74% to 100%
2026
IRDAI releases a public consultation paper on
major reforms to the insurance distribution
system

incentives to sell products and inappropriate sales practices. This should shore up the credibility of the 
insurance market and the foundation for its sustainable development. 
On the minus side, there are worries about side effects arising from the transition. As rules on selling 
become stricter, insurance companies and the financial institutions acting as insurance agents will inevitably 
see some temporary moderation in the pace of their earnings growth. Moreover, given that insurance 
companies and financial institutions are major investors in India's financial markets, tighter regulation is 
likely to exert downward pressure on equity and bond prices. 
These changes in the insurance market provide an important perspective for assessing the future 
trajectory of the Indian economy. They illustrate that rapid market expansion driven by liberalization can 
create a variety of problems. And this dynamic is not limited to the insurance sector, as it can also occur 
across many other industries. At the same time, the redesign and recalibration of regulations in response to 
such developments can provide a foundation for a new stage of growth. The new insurance regulations 
could constitute an early example of this phenomenon, so will be closely watched to see what changes they 
bring in the years ahead.  
  (Shotaro Kumagai)

The Japan Research Institute, Limited

4 
Topics  Talent travel curbs may weigh on China’s growth

In September, the Chinese government introduced new exit-entry rules. Indicative of an effort to retain talent in order 
to prevent technology leakage, the rules could narrow opportunities for China to absorb overseas knowledge and weigh 
on its economic growth over the medium to long term.

■ Scope of overseas travel restrictions expanded 
On September 15, the Chinese 
government brought in new rules 
specifying conditions and screening 
procedures for entry and exit. The new 
rules explicitly state that the Ministry of 
Commerce or other departments may 
prohibit Chinese citizens from leaving the 
country if they violate export-control or 
technology import/export regulations and 
are deemed likely to threaten national 
industrial or technological security.  
Under China's Exit and Entry 
Administration Law, which came into 
effect in 2013, defendants and suspects in 
criminal cases, as well as persons involved 
in unsettled civil cases whom courts have 
prohibited from leaving the country, had 
already been subject to exit restrictions. In 
addition, Chinese citizens could be denied 
permission to leave the country if the 
competent departments determined that 
their departure might endanger national 
security or interests. Aside from formal exit 
bans, personnel who handle state secrets 
and officials in the public sector have long 
been subject to controls such as centralized 
passport management and the need to 
obtain approval in advance of overseas travel. What the new rules effectively do is make it clear that exit 
can also be restricted when there are issues from the standpoint of economic security. 
Against this backdrop, it was reported at the end of September that overseas travel restrictions already 
imposed on key personnel in the AI and semiconductor sectors had been expanded to include their spouses, 
children, and other family members. According to these reports, these key personnel include prominent AI 
startup founders, senior executives at major private enterprises, and core R&D talent. Although the measure 
does not constitute a blanket travel ban, affected individuals and their families are reportedly required to 
obtain government approval for exit even in the case of short private trips or business travel. 
This regulatory tightening is thought to be related to the technological rivalry between the U.S. and China. 
As competition surrounding critical technologies such as generative AI intensifies, the Chinese government 
appears to view not only technologies themselves, but also the researchers and engineers who create them, 
as strategic assets. By widening travel curbs to encompass family members, the authorities are likely 
seeking to prevent overseas emigration by individuals or the relocation of R&D operations abroad. A 
noteworthy aspect of the recent reports is that the controls apparently now extend beyond the public sector 
to key personnel at private enterprises, as well as their families. Restrictions like these on talent mobility 
could undermine the mechanisms of technology absorption and productivity improvement that have 
hitherto facilitated China's growth.

ASIA MONTHLY REPORT  November 2026, No.308

The Japan Research Institute, Limited

5 
■ China's productivity gains have been supported by market opening and technology 
absorption 
Productivity exerts a substantial influence on 
economic growth. Total factor productivity (TFP), an 
indicator of an economy's overall production 
efficiency, provides an estimate of efficiency in 
production that cannot be explained solely in terms of 
inputs of capital and labor, and reflects factors such as 
levels of technology and efficiency of resource 
allocation. 
China's TFP has been rising for many years. In 
addition to the transition to a market economy and the 
growth of private enterprises since the country adopted 
the policy of reform and opening up, Chinese 
productivity has benefitted from the absorption of 
foreign technology and managerial know‑how through 
trade and direct investment. A labor shift from 
agriculture to manufacturing and other sectors, along 
with competition among firms and the weeding out of 
weaker players, has also helped deliver productivity 
gains across the economy.  
Nevertheless, China's TFP remains low relative to 
other major economies. According to estimates from 
Penn World Table, China's TFP in 2023 was less than 
half that of the U.S., and it was also below the levels not 
only of Germany and other major European economies 
and of South Korea and Japan, but of Thailand and 
Malaysia, too. Factors behind this include inefficient 
resource allocation and the slow diffusion of advanced 
technologies and management practices. Preferential 
financial and institutional treatment for state‑owned 
enterprises, regional protectionism, and mechanisms 
that impede market entry and exit have distorted the 
allocation of resources, preventing capital and labor 
from being fully utilized by more productive firms and sectors. Moreover, although China has achieved 
strong competitiveness in certain advanced industries, such as electric vehicles and semiconductors, the 
leading-edge technologies and management practices developed in these sectors have not sufficiently 
diffused to the service sector and small and medium‑sized enterprises, which form the foundation of the 
economy, and this has constrained overall productivity growth.  
■ Moves to contain technology could hinder growth over the medium to long term 
Efforts to retain talent in order to prevent technology leakage could further delay China's productivity 
catch‑up with advanced economies. While restrictions on overseas travel by elite professionals may help 
with the retention of domestic R&D talent and protection of current technological advantages, they could 
also reduce opportunities to absorb overseas knowledge and to jointly develop new technologies with 
foreign partners. The transfer of technology and know‑how does not occur solely through the sharing of 
"explicit knowledge," in the form of academic papers, technical blueprints, and so on. "Tacit knowledge," 
which is transmitted through face‑to‑face discussion, collaborative work, and the acquisition of experience 
in production settings, is also crucial. If travel controls delay or prevent participation in international 
conferences, joint research projects, and technology exchanges with overseas companies, opportunities to 
absorb such knowledge may be lost, slowing technological innovation and improvements in production 
processes. Furthermore, if obtaining travel approval becomes more time‑consuming or less predictable, the 
overseas assignment or international deployment of technical personnel could also be obstructed. 
The new rules also cover the entry of foreign nationals. If uncertainty surrounding the administration of 
entry and exit controls increases, it could create obstacles to attracting and retaining highly skilled talent 
from abroad. At a time when inefficient resource allocation and the slow diffusion of advanced knowledge 
are already weighing on productivity growth in China, curbs on people-to- people exchange could further

impede both the absorption and creation of knowledge, as well as its practical application. Although the 
magnitude of the impact will depend on the scope of application and actual implementation on the ground, 
restrictions on talent exchanges and overseas travel that reflect excessive emphasis on technology 
protection could slow China's TFP growth over the medium to long term and act as a headwind to the 
Chinese economy by lowering its potential growth rate. 
(Hiromu Komiya)

2 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Oil Price Dynamics Remain Key to the Outlook
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
Inflation, interest rates and equity market dynamics have changed materially since mid-
August, as widening attacks on Middle East and Russian crude oil and refining capacity
worsened supply and price conditions. Indeed, with the oil market already strained, an
early-September attack on a major Saudi Arabian export pipeline sharply raised concerns

about a protracted and deepening oil crisis. While Middle East crude exports eventually
managed to significantly rebound, increased security risks and freight costs raised
physical crude prices.
Against this backdrop, the spot Brent price surged from $92/barrel in mid-August
back into a $115 to $135/barrel price range until recently. Reflecting unusual market
tightness, other North Sea oil grades were recently trading even higher, up to $160/
barrel, according to the International Energy Agency (IEA). Even as it cooled from $135

to $115/barrel between October 2 and October 20, spot Brent is still almost double
year-ago levels, and so are many diesel prices. The AAA average U.S. retail diesel price
recently hit a new record and was 74% higher year-over-year (YoY) as of October 20.
With insufficient regional supply, the New York Harbor wholesale diesel price is 130%
higher from a year ago, according to the Energy Information Administration (EIA).
Indeed, sharply higher oil prices and a growing diesel shortage have put the global diesel

market under extreme strain. The U.S., for example, has little distillate cushion and
limited spare refining capacity. The distillate inventory (diesel and fuel oil) has decreased
from 37 days of demand coverage in January to just 26 days by early October, according
to the EIA. This 30% drop brought the supply buffer to the bottom of its historical
range. With limited refining capacity and reliance on imports for about 10% of regional
consumption, East Coast distillate inventories are the lowest since 1990, for example,

and are expected to remain below their 5-year average into late 2027, according to EIA
data. More broadly, according to the IEA’s October Oil Market Report (OMR), “oil market
balances are forecast to remain in deficit through the end of the year, with constrained
middle distillate supplies likely to persist well into 2027.”
Thus, although wage growth/labor demand do not appear inflationary, the oil crisis
risks continuing to feed “core” inflation. According to the Federal Reserve’s Beige Book1

covering precisely the mid-August to early-October period of heightened oil-market
turmoil, “higher oil prices permeated all District reports,” with firms noting that fuel
surcharges were passing on increased costs throughout the supply chain. The 22% rise
in airfares over the past year alone contributed around 0.2 percentage point (pp) to the
2.6% 12-month increase in the “core” consumer price index (CPI) for September. Aside
from transportation costs effects, the spike in diesel prices also portends high food

prices with a few-month lag given planting/harvesting cost considerations.
By solidifying expectations for higher Fed interest rates to fight inflation, the surge in oil
prices has contributed to the spike in long-term Treasury yields this year. According to an
October 13, 2026, Empirical Research Partners report, the Iran-war oil crisis is estimated
to have contributed about 0.50 pp to the roughly 2.40 pp increase in the real U.S. long-
term Treasury rate from 2020 to early October (Exhibit 1A). According to the report, this

oil-price effect accounts for “much of the rise in real rates since February 27, 2026...”
On the positive side, while most other underpinnings for higher rates compared to the
pre-pandemic decade are structural (Exhibit 1A), the oil-price-related effect on interest
rates is reversible, implying that a resolution to the current oil crisis has the potential to
help lower both inflation and interest rates, relieving some of the current headwinds to
growth and asset prices.

Indeed, while much of the increase in interest rates since the pandemic reflects stronger,
more normal economic growth, and has thus been accompanied by surging corporate
1 Summary of Commentary on Current Economic Conditions by Federal Reserve District, Federal Reserve, October 2026.

earnings and risk-asset prices, worsening oil-market conditions since mid-summer and
associated spike in yields have been accompanied by a loss of momentum in U.S. equity
prices (Exhibit 1B). Given their higher sensitivity to energy price and interest rates shocks,
Small-caps lost relatively more momentum than Large-caps during this period.
The Large-cap rally also faded, though, as oil and interest rate headwinds widened stock-

level return dispersion, with roughly 40% of constituent stocks in bear market territory
(down 20% or more). Still, buoyed by the Technology sector as well as by expectations
of conflict resolution, the market-capitalization weighted S&P 500 Index has mainly
consolidated just below its peak. While the equal-weighted S&P 500 Index stumbled more,
it was just about 3% off its peak as of October 20.
While the economy remains resilient and profit expectations remain strong, persistent

oil market turmoil makes the macro backdrop more fragile. For example, the Beige Book
not only highlights concern about spreading fuel-related inflation, as noted above, but its
language also suggests that economic activity lost some momentum since its mid-July
to late-August report. Overall, it describes growth as slight to moderate, with generally
moderate manufacturing growth except for robust activity in the energy-heavy Dallas
District. Little growth is reported in other sectors overall, with most Districts seeing little

to no change in consumer spending, for example.
Employment was described as stable to little changed, with generally adequate labor supply
except for certain specialized trade workers. Five of twelve Districts note some reports of
businesses cutting staff through attrition, delaying hiring or anticipating less job creation
as a result of AI-driven productivity gains. Most Districts reported that business maintained
a positive outlook for coming months, though “expectations tend to be for slight growth,

weighted toward manufacturing and burdened by persistent uncertainty.”
The September employment and retail sales reports seem to validate the anecdotal Beige
Book evidence. Also, the 0.4% annualized manufacturing production growth over the July to
September period (and 1.1% YoY, according to the Federal Reserve Broad), while welcome,
is hardly an indication of booming conditions. Against a backdrop of rising electricity prices,
wage growth lagging inflation, and higher gasoline prices broadly offsetting this year’s tax

cut benefits, real consumer spending is unlikely to accelerate much absent stronger labor
demand and real wage growth. A resolution to the oil crisis that brings economic relief and
tempers interest rates is thus critical for continued expansion in 2027, in our view.

Investment Implications
Portfolio maintenance is a key
component of successful investing.
It’s time for investors to assess their
portfolios—or look under the hood.
MARKET VIEW
Six Questions Investors Should Be Asking Themselves Right Now
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
After four years of rising equity prices, investors have plenty to celebrate. Despite

inflation scares, geopolitical tensions, trade wars, rising interest rates and breathless
predictions around AI, the S&P 500 has returned some 131% (total returns) since the
bull market in equities commenced in October 2022. Corporate earnings have generally
exceeded expectations; AI has ignited a historic investment boom, and America’s
technology giants have powered the market to new heights.
We believe the bull will rumble on into 2027. We also believe that now is the time for

investors to take stock of their portfolios, since bull markets have a way of changing the
composition of portfolios. In addition, investors should not assume that the investment
strategies that worked well in the past four years will necessarily deliver comparable
results during the next four. Below are six questions investors should be asking
themselves right now:
Number One: How concentrated is my portfolio? Many investors believe they are
invested in the stock market when, in fact, they are invested in a handful of companies—

specifically AI-related names that have soared and dominated the index over the past
few years. Information technology now accounts for roughly 40% of the market cap of
the S&P 500, while the top 10 companies represent a share of 38.4%.
As the Financial Times recently noted, U.S. equity and credit markets have become so
increasingly concentrated in AI-related groups that “more than two-thirds of companies
in the Russell 1000 index of large U.S. groups are linked to AI, either directly or

indirectly or because the technology is becoming embedded in their business models.”2
Meanwhile, Big Technology hyperscalers and companies that are beneficiaries of the AI
spending boom make up about 16% of the U.S. high-grade bond market after a huge
borrowing binge, making the grouping the largest in the investment-grade bond market.2
The upshot: Investors that have been rewarded by concentration need to ask themselves
what percentage of their wealth is tied to the all-encompassing AI trade and consider

what would happen if mega-cap tech leadership falters.
Number Two: How diversified is my portfolio? While concentration asks whether
investors own too much of a few assets, diversification asks whether investors own enough
of different assets that behave differently when economic cycles or financial conditions
change. A portfolio containing hundreds of U.S. Equities via exchange-traded funds, mutual
funds and other assets may appear diversified. But if those companies are overwhelmingly

exposed to the same economic cycle, interest rate environment, or technology theme,
the diversification benefits are diluted. Case in point: While we remain constructive on
Emerging Market (EM) Equities, investors need to understand that a significant driver of
EM returns has come from AI-driven markets like Taiwan and South Korea.
Today, true diversification means considering exposure to hard assets (commodities, real
estate), EM mining and agricultural leaders, EM bonds and currencies, China bio-technology,

Small-cap international, and Private Equity and Hedge Funds for qualified investors.
Number Three: How tax-efficient is my portfolio? Bull markets create wealth—in
addition to tax liabilities. The good news is that after four years of rising equity returns,
investors are sitting on sizable, unrealized capital gains. The bad news: Market gains
can feel like a financial straitjacket, making it difficult to rebalance portfolios without
triggering significant tax liabilities.

Against this backdrop, now is the time for investors to review where the assets are
held—distinguishing between taxable and tax-advantaged accounts, and the attendant tax
ramifications. The appropriate mix depends on individual circumstances, including future
2 “Investors seek shelter from heightened exposure to AI”, The Financial Times, October 6, 2026.

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
withdrawal taxes. It is also time to consider tax-loss strategies, charitable donations of
securities and other tactics and strategies since in the end, the measure of investment
success isn’t simply how much money investors make. It’s how much they keep.
Number Four: Is it time to take profits?* There is a peculiarity to bull markets: The
more money investors make, the more the attendant tax liabilities. Tax liabilities, in turn,

can discourage selling, and the reluctance to sell can leave investors dangerously exposed
to yesterday’s leaders and compound concentration risks. That brings up an emotionally
difficult decision: When should investors sell their leaders or take some chips off the table?
The question is notably relevant today, given four years of substantial Equity gains.
Beyond tax considerations, investors need to understand that profit-taking can create
financial flexibility. Realized after-tax gains can replenish cash reserves, fund near-

term spending needs, reduce leverage or most importantly, provide capital to rebalance
portfolios in other areas of the market. In general, the goal of taking profits isn’t to get
out of the market—it is to ensure that after years of accumulated gains, these proceeds
are redeployed and redistributed across various asset classes that continue to support
and underpin long-term wealth accumulation.
Number Five: How well is my portfolio positioned for a new interest rate

regime? For much of the past quarter century, investors operated in a world of copious
liquidity, declining interest rates and relatively cheap capital. The era of capital abundance
favored stocks over bonds, but that era is over—we are shifting to a world of capital
scarcity, or intense competition for capital and the attendant hike in the cost of capital.
In this world, higher rates weigh the most on long-duration assets. Positioning for this
regime favors companies with strong balance sheets and cash flow over firms with heavy

debt or refinancing needs. High-quality bonds are more attractive in this environment;
sovereign debt risks are concentrated more overseas (i.e., France) than the U.S.
Number Six: How well is my portfolio positioned for the next shock? Shocks
happen. If the past four years have taught investors anything, it is that the unexpected
should be the expected. This decade alone has produced gut-wrenching market moves
related to a global pandemic, a ground war in Europe, the reconfiguration of global

supply chains, epic technological change and a war in the Middle East. Through it all, U.S.
Equities have trended higher but hardly in a linear fashion.
The next shocks could emerge in the global bond market, where mounting government
debt and higher borrowing costs have triggered mounting volatility. It could emerge from
disappointing returns on massive AI investments. Or it could come from a geopolitical
event, another energy shock or any other unforeseen event. Investors can’t predict the

next shock, but they can prepare for it. So now is the time for investors to stress test
their portfolios against a potential shock, examining how a steep downdraft in Equities
or spike in bond yields would affect their retirement plans, liquidity needs and other
financial goals and objectives.
Exhibit 2: Six Questions to Stress Test Your Portfolio.
The six questions What investors should examine Portfolio diagnostic
1. How concentrated am I? Mega-cap technology exposure and overlapping holdings % of portfolio in top 10 stocks

2. Am I truly diversified? Asset allocation, geographic mix and correlated exposures Current equity/bond mix versus target
3. How tax-efficient am I? Unrealized gains, asset location and tax-loss harvesting Embedded capital gains and estimated tax cost of rebalancing
4. Is it time to take profits? Portfolio drift, oversized winners and valuations Positions exceeding target allocation
5. Am I ready for higher interest rates? Duration, leverage, refinancing risks and cash flows Bond duration and exposure to highly leveraged companies

6 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THE TICKER TAPE
What are rising credit spreads signaling about the broader market environment?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Officer
Rising French bond yields with a sharp rise in spreads above German bunds, mixed
with AI buildout concerns, are creating nervousness across the capital markets. This
nervousness centers around the ability to fund deficits, the potential for slower growth

and the future return on the announced capital investment. We expect these concerns
to continue to linger well into next year as growth worries remain in France, bond
yields stay sticky around current levels, and, in the case of AI, investors demand more
compensation for financing.
At this stage, we do not expect the rising spreads in targeted areas to be a precursor
for a material slowdown in economic growth overall. Rather, we believe this is a natural

signal that investors are moving up the quality stack as we move into the middle of
the AI buildout. The bottom line is that rising credit spreads in lower quality corners of
the market are natural along the lifecycle of financing. When free cash flow is called
into question, or when debt financing costs begin to approach the expected return on
investment, investors begin to reprice risk.
We continue to emphasize higher quality balance sheets in corporate and sovereign

credit as well as higher quality leaders in the AI buildout overall. The closer we get to
2027, investors will likely begin to transition from too much exposure to the “buildout”
and not enough exposure to “AI adopters.”

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
SS&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

Consumer Price Index is a statistical measure that tracks the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a representative "market basket" of everyday goods and
services.
S&P 500 Market-cap Weighted Index is a float-adjusted market-capitalization-weighted index, meaning a company's influence and percentage weight in the index are determined by its
total market value (share price multiplied by the number of outstanding shares).

S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Index is a variation of the traditional S&P 500 where every single company in the index is given the exact same weight—0.2%—regardless of its size or market
capitalization.
S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Industrials Index imposes equal weights on the index constituents included in the S&P 500 that are classified in the GICS® industrials sector.
Russell 1000 Index is a stock market index that tracks the performance of the 1,000 largest publicly traded companies in the United States.

Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

Document 294: Central banks_ a pause while waiting for more information (2026-10-29)

Date: 2026-10-29

### Page 1

Paris, 30 October 2026

EcoFlash

Central banks: a pause while waiting for more information

As expected, the Fed, the ECB, and the BoJ left their rates unchanged this week. This pause, however, does not mark 
the end of the cycle. The ECB’s tone is becoming more balanced, weighing inflationary risks against tightening financial 
conditions, but one final rate hike in December remains our base case. Across the Atlantic, Kevin Warsh describes an 
environment conducive to further tightening, while remaining cautious about the risk of a credit shock. In Japan, the 
lasting exit from the deflationary cycle is taking shape, and monetary policy adjustment is expected to continue 
gradually. We anticipate two rate hikes by the Fed by January and four by the BoJ by the second half of 2028.

Eurozone: A more balanced tone, but the course remains unchanged. The ECB’s statement marks a shift from September. 
It now explicitly acknowledges both upside risks to inflation (the magnitude and persistence of the energy shock) and downside 
pressures (tightening financial conditions). The statement that inflation would remain “well above target for an extended period” 
no longer appears. In essence, however, there is little change. A rate hike in December remains our base case scenario, widely 
anticipated and implicitly signaled by Christine Lagarde during the Q&A (“If the current situation is not called into question by the 
next set of economic data, the path is clear”). Lagarde presented the rise in sovereign yields as a global phenomenon that is 
tightening financial conditions; regarding the TPI, she stuck to the usual stance: the ECB will intervene if monetary policy 
transmission were to be hampered “by disorderly movements not justified by fundamentals”. By December, the indirect and 
second-round effects of the energy shock will be closely scrutinised. From our perspective, September’s producer prices offer 
clearer signals, but the increase remains limited to a few sectors: energy, electricity-intensive industries and electrical equipment, 
driven by IA1. For now, companies in the Eurozone, particularly in the services sector, appear to be absorbing the shock. The 
European Commission’s survey released this Thursday indicates a rise in the sales price index in October, which remains in line 
with recent months.

United States: The Fed navigates between risks. The FOMC pauses after September’s rate hike and maintains the federal 
funds target at 3.75%-4.0%. However, Fed Chairman Kevin Warsh outlined a landscape conducive to further tightening: stronger 
growth, “rather positive” labor market conditions, and, above all, inflation that is “too high.” As a result, two dissenting votes (out 
of 12) were cast in favor of a 25bps hike. But the committee also appears to fear the adverse consequences of tightening too 
quickly, which is why it is waiting to “accumulate more information.” AI is increasingly at the heart of monetary policymaking. In 
particular, K. Warsh warned against the risks of an abrupt tightening of interest rate conditions, including for the AI investment 
cycle - which triggered a steepening of the yield curve. He also announced that he would soon (on November 16) attend a hearing 
of the “Productivity and Employment” task force to “refine his views” on the implications of AI for the dual mandate. Governor Lisa 
Cook, who voted for a 25bps hike, had noted ahead of the committee meeting “ongoing inflationary pressures” and a risk that they 
might not “subside quickly”. In addition, K. Warsh announced that there would be fewer meetings resulting in rate decisions: 7 in 
2027 and 6 in 2028, compared with 8 today. The change is expected to take effect starting in the fall of 2027. The meetings freed 
up by this change would allow the committee to focus on “strategic deliberations” and long-term issues.

Japan: A status quo, but a tightening cycle set to continue as the economy emerges from deflation. The Bank of Japan 
decided, in line with our forecasts and the consensus, to keep its policy rate target steady at 1.25% at its October 29-30 meeting. 
One dissenting vote called for a rate hike (25bps), contrasting with the two dovish dissenting votes (opposed to a rate hike) at the 
previous meeting. As in the US, the decision is justified by the need to further analyse risks to economic activity, prices, and 
financial conditions. Above all, the central bank’s outlook for economic activity and prices marks a turning point: it explicitly states 
that “core inflation has reached a level generally in line with price stability” and that it is “expected to remain there.” Governor Ueda 
refined this by noting that it is now necessary to “anchor” inflation at this level before declaring the target achieved. Furthermore, 
the emphasis on “upside risks to prices” and the fact that any rise in core inflation is now deemed “undesirable” indicate that 
monetary tightening will continue.

The monetary tightening cycle continues. The next steps are likely to be more significant in the United States, where inflation 
has exceeded the target for nearly six years and coexists with a favorable outlook for growth and employment. In the Eurozone, 
the rise in inflation is primarily due to a supply shock in energy costs (though it is also fueled by demand in the United States). It 
continues to have little impact on measures of core inflation, including those tracked by the ECB, such as the HICP; and 
households’ long-term inflation expectations remain anchored. The degree of monetary tightening also differs. In real terms, the 
ECB’s current rates (the deposit facility rate deflated by the core CPI) hover around 0%, a level that more or less corresponds to 
estimates of the natural rate. The Fed, meanwhile, remains in accommodative territory: the real rate (Fed Funds deflated by Core

1 This is a point underscored by the first PPI releases for September (Spain, Italy).

### Page 2

PCE) is at around 0.8%, 40bps below the committee’s implied long-term level (1.2%). Japan remains an outlier: the rise in rates 
preceded the inflationary shock and, until now, has been more a matter of normalization. It is therefore more extensive and spread 
out over time. The central bank is adjusting to the new inflation regime and remains cautious in its exit from accommodative 
policies. The starting point is significantly lower, with real rates still negative, but the adjustment cycle should ultimately bring it to 
the upper end of the neutral range estimated by the BoJ (1.1%-2.5%). We therefore anticipate one final rate hike by the ECB in 
December, two more hikes by the Fed, first in December and then in January, and four spaced-out hikes by the BoJ through the 
second half of 2028.

2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
All state Housing Sentiment Indexes are at weak levels.
However, November saw a bigger decline in Western
Australia (–4.8%mth) which now has the weakest housing-
related sentiment index across the major states at just 80.9.
Queensland and South Australia moved in line with the

national index. Moves were notably milder in New South
Wales (–1.7%) and especially Victoria (–0.7%) where the state
index, at 86.6, is the least pessimistic of the major states.
The sub-group detail showed a more pronounced weakening in
housing-related sentiment amongst high income earners and
consumers aged over 55 with sentiment holding about steady
amongst low-income earners, albeit at weaker overall levels.
Our November consumer sentiment survey also included

two new regular questions on consumers’ property buying
and selling intentions over the next twelve months. These
questions will continue to be run every month.
Despite the broader pessimism around housing, a relatively
high number of consumers are planning to buy in 2027.
Responses show 12.8% of consumers plan to buy a new
home over the next year. Comparable figures from Westpac’s
Home Ownership survey going back to 2019 suggest this is a
relatively firm result, up slightly since the middle of the year,

on par with the start of 2025 and above the levels reported
during the COVID pandemic in 2020-21. The result suggests
there is significant ‘latent demand’ for home purchases in
Australia, and that many buyers are just waiting for the right
opportunity. The state detail shows intentions are particularly
high in Queensland where one in five consumers is reportedly
planning to buy in 2027.
Interestingly, the positive intentions are not just around home

purchase with 11.2% of consumers reporting that they plan
to buy an investment property over the next year. Again,
comparable figures from Westpac’s Home Ownership survey
show this is up a tick compared to May and only marginally
below the 11.5% reporting intentions to buy investment
properties back in January 2025.
BULLETIN
The Westpac Housing Sentiment Index fell 2.2% to 83.2 in
November from 85.3 in October.
The Index is a composite based on housing-related sentiment

measures from the Westpac-Melbourne Institute Consumer
Sentiment survey. It already appears regularly in Westpac’s
Housing Pulse reports but will now be published on a
monthly basis. The Index has proven to be an extremely useful
predictor of turning points in housing markets, both nationally
and across states. Westpac is also introducing two new
survey questions on consumers property buying and selling
intentions that will be run every month.
The November update shows housing-related sentiment

remains remain deeply pessimistic. At 85.3, the latest Index
read is in the bottom 3% of observations since 2005, when all
components first became available. That said, it is still above the
81.4 low in late 2022 and the 78.6 registered when the COVID
pandemic hit in 2020. Housing-related sentiment was markedly
weaker during the slump in the late 1980s/early 1990s.
Component-wise, the November sentiment decline centred on
a sharp downgrade to price expectations. As detailed below,

the Westpac Housing Sentiment Index composite is based
on four sub-indexes: one tracking assessments of ‘time to buy
a dwelling’; one tracking ‘consumer house price expectations’;
one tracking consumer expectations for unemployment and a
measure of risk aversion based on consumer assessments of
the ‘wisest place for savings’.
Consumer price expectations took a big step down in
November. The Westpac–Melbourne Institute Index of
House Price Expectations recorded a steep 13.4% drop

to 99.6, marking the second biggest monthly deterioration
since the COVID period and the first net negative (sub-100)
read since November 2022. The detailed responses show
consumers are now evenly split on the outlook, just over 38%
expecting more declines, 19% expecting no change and 38%
expecting a rise (5% reported “don’t know).
At least some consumers are starting to see lower prices as
a potential buying opportunity. The ‘time to buy a dwelling’

index rose 1.3% to 89.6 in November, to be up 1.3% on a year
ago as well but still at a very weak level overall, well below
the long run average of 114.
The wider economic backdrop was also less conducive for
housing-related sentiment. Consumers continue to report
a gradual rise in job-loss fears and risk aversion remains
elevated near historic highs. Both factors are likely to be
deterring prospective home buyers.
Housing sentiment sinks but buying intentions positive

“Despite the broader pessimism
around housing, a relatively high
number of consumers are planning
to buy ...”

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Conversely, just 8.2% of Australians are planning to sell a home 
over the next year. Comparable figures from Westpac’s Home 
Ownership survey suggest this is down significantly from 10.3% 
back in May and near the 7.7% low recorded at the start of 2024. 
On investment properties, 6.7% of all those surveyed reported 
plans to sell. However, only 23% of consumers reported 
owning an investment property – as a share of this sub-
group, the proportion planning to sell is just over 26%. While 
it is unclear whether this is relatively high (no comparable 
measures are available from Westpac’s Home Ownership 
survey), a third of this same sub-group reported an intention to 
buy an investment property over the next year, indicating that 
amongst investment property owners prospective buyers still 
outnumber prospective sellers. 
Overall, the consumer views on housing show a clear tension. 
On the one side, high interest rates, a continued slide in prices 
and unease about a riskier outlook for jobs and the wider 
economy are weighing heavily on housing-related sentiment. 
But against this, relatively few are planning to sell and there 
is a relatively high proportion of consumers looking to buy. 
This suggests that conditions could recover quite quickly if and 
when the macro-economic backdrop becomes less threatening 
and the market starts to stabilise.

About the Westpac Housing Sentiment Index 
The Westpac Housing Sentiment Index is a composite 
measure based on a weighted combination of four indexes 
from the Westpac–Melbourne Institute Consumer 
Sentiment survey. Two of these are ‘primary’ components 
with a higher weight that relate directly to consumer 
perceptions of housing market conditions: the Westpac–
Melbourne Institute ‘time to buy a dwelling’ index and the 
Westpac–Melbourne Institute House Price Expectations 
Index. The remaining ‘supplementary’ components, with 
lower weights, relate to consumer assessments of job 
security – the Westpac–Melbourne Institute Unemployment 
Expectations Index – and risk appetite – the Westpac Risk 
Aversion Index.
Each of these components is seasonally adjusted, converted 
to a consistent base and combined using fixed weights 
determined by historical regression analysis. Note that the 
house price expectations component is only available from 
2009 on – a re-weighted composite based on the remaining 
measures is used for earlier periods.  
The resulting composite measures provide significant insight 
into housing market conditions both nationally and at the 
individual state level. The national index has over 50yrs of 
history and a clear lead indicator relationship with a variety 
of housing market metrics. The index is particularly good at 
picking turning points in housing market turnover – correctly 
anticipating every major upswing and downturn since 1980 
with a lead of around three months (four once the timeliness 
of sentiment updates is included).
